#### 60. Siwei Fan — Cryogenic Propellant Gauging in Low-Gravity (ERT/EIT) ⭐⭐⭐ STRATEGIC (dual-use for Exos's own cryo program)
- **Segment:** University research → BLK3 payload + potential technology collaboration
- **PRIMARY POC (engaged):** **Siwei Fan** — Embry-Riddle Aeronautical University (theory/algorithms lead). **Email: FANS2@erau.edu**. Added to Supabase CRM 2026-08-19 (owner Lizi, stage Engaged).
- **Collaborator / connector:** **Dr. Yanda** — hands-on electronics, safety, standards; referred Siwei to Lizi (Lizi had a prior meeting with Dr. Yanda). Institution confirmed: **Embry-Riddle Aeronautical University**.
- **Call held:** 2026-08-19 (Lizi + Siwei Fan).
- **Project:** Non-invasive gauging of **cryogenic propellant in low gravity** — electrodes line the propellant tank interior (flexible printed-PCB electrodes), readings + algorithm reconstruct the propellant distribution (analogous to MRI/CT — electrical resistance/capacitance tomography). Solves a real unsolved problem: measuring floating/bubbly cryo propellant in microgravity. Tech itself is mature on Earth (grain/fluid-flow in pipes); **no good demonstration exists in actual low gravity** — all lab results. They have a preliminary dewar lab setup; have LN2 but haven't run the full low-temp test; a more compact build now.
- **Credibility:** **1st place in a NASA national competition** (on NASA's website); met NASA experts in Huntsville (MSFC) ~18 months ago.
- **Funding:** **No active NASA funding** currently. Has some prior internal campus funding (built the current device). Open door: Lizi to hunt matching solicitations and **co-write a funding proposal** with them.
- **Why BLK3 is a near-perfect fit:** Exos flies **cryogenic propellant (LOx/ethanol)** in **Type-5 tanks (no metal liner)** — an ideal, real cryo environment for the demo; ~2-3 min microgravity, hardware + data recovered within the hour; iterative reflight (Mon → ~5-day turn → next flight) lets them fix and re-fly fast.
- **⭐ STRATEGIC DUAL-USE (surface to Jon):** This gauging tech is **directly adjacent to Exos's OWN in-space propellant-transfer/gauging program** (NASA orbital liquid-transfer proposal OPP-246 + BLK3 F1 LOx-transfer demo + Scorpius Type-5 cryo heritage). It could **instrument Exos's own cryo tanks / transfer demo** — this is a payload customer AND a potential technology collaborator, not just a bay sale. Consider a joint proposal where Exos = flight platform + cryo host and Fan/Yanda = gauging payload.
- **Integration note:** electrodes must contact the propellant → inside the tank → some tank modification/interface needed (assess feasibility; may be better demoed in a dedicated instrumented cryo tank than the flight propellant tanks).
- **NDA:** Lizi offered an NDA if needed before he sends sensitive material.
- **Agreed next steps:**
  1. **Siwei sends** (today/tomorrow after seeing Dr. Yanda): a couple-page write-up — background, theory, estimated **dimensions + mass** — plus project materials, names of Siwei + Dr. Yanda, and the NASA-competition link. ("Will upload docs once he sends them" — Lizi, 2026-08-19.)
  2. **Lizi:** share with team → produce a **pricing estimate**; **dig for matching solicitations** to co-write a funding proposal; flag the dual-use cryo angle to Jon.
- **EXPORT:** University research → marketing/PUG releasable; but **cryogenic propellant-management / tank-instrumentation is export-sensitive (ITAR/EAR)** — any technical-data exchange, ICD/CAD/FEA, or tank-interface detail routes through Jon + export screen before anything leaves the Payload User Guide. Confirm Siwei's citizenship/institution status for any controlled data.
- **Owner:** Lizi. **Status:** ENGAGED — awaiting his docs, then price + solicitation hunt. Add to CRM once email received. Logged to MC opp PAY-3121.

Funds flight testing of space tech on commercial suborbital/orbital providers; 2022 cycle was up to **$750K**, ~12 awards, no cost share. **Structure: ERAU/Fan = tech PI, Exos = named flight provider (BLK3).** This literally funds flying the ECT gauging demo on BLK3.
